=== [Section]
Preparation

=== [Input]

=== [Subsection]
Others\LineSeparator

=== [Input]
knotparam[f_]:=f/.{a->-T*A^2,q->Q^2T^2,t->Q^2};

=== [Input]
gfactor[f_]:=Module[{n,m,x0=Simplify[Expand[f],Q>0&&T>0&&A>0],x1,x2,x3,ans,sol=0},expa=Exponent[x0,A]+1;m=Exponent[x0,A,Min]-1;expq=Exponent[x0,Q]/2+1;While[m<expa,x1=Coefficient[x0,A,m];n=expq;ans=0;While[n>0,x2=x1-x((Q-Q^(-1))(Q T-T^(-1)Q^(-1)))^(n);x3=Solve[Coefficient[x2,Q,Exponent[x2,Q]]==0,x][[1]]//Simplify//Expand;x1=x2/.x3;ans=ans+x g^(n)/.x3;(*Print[x3]*);n=n-1;\IndentingNewLine];x1=Simplify[x1];(*Print[ans]*);sol=sol+A^(m)(x1+ans);m=m+1];Return[Collect[sol//Factor,A]]];

=== [Input]
gformqt[f_]:=Module[{m,x0=Simplify[f,Q>0&&T>0&&A>0],x2=0,expa},expa=Exponent[x0,A]+1;m=Exponent[x0,A,Min]-1;While[m<expa,x1=Collect[Coefficient[x0,A,m]T^(-m/2)(-1)^(m/2)//Simplify//Expand,g]/.T->(s)^(1/2);x2=x2+x1*a^(m/2);m=m+1];Return[x2]]
